cca5bf4b919d3be83a3822d71e4ef99e8be7553a [ diff ] 
Pass UniversalOptOutService when checking settings visibility.

Previously, nullptr was passed as the service to GetUniversalOptOutState
in PrivacyTableViewController. This updates
ShouldShowUniversalOptOutSettings to retrieve and pass the profile's
UniversalOptOutService instance.

UniversalOptOutService is used to check
overall eligibility of the user including their signed-in and signed-out
states, if this is null we fall back to the prefs check which only
covers the signed out state.

Fixed: 574322632

d5c78a9311d3e47ba392ab91ac26b0b64e8fd646 [ diff ] 
Skip 720p 1.5 Mbps VBR WebCodecs rate control tests on Pixel 10/11

Pixel 10 and Pixel 11 hardware encoders enforce a minimum 720p VBR
bitrate floor of 2.2 Mbps (AVC) and 2.0 Mbps (HEVC), so 1.5 Mbps VBR
cannot be met.

Bug: 447317875, 558993697

[HiC] Make canvas break out of the enclosing decorating box
📋 Metadata

4603aa286a52f6f69ea937290a7147f013be7970
[ log ]
author
Stefan Zager <szager@chromium.org>

committer
Chromium LUCI CQ <chromium-scoped@luci-project-accounts.iam.gserviceaccount.com>
Sat Oct 17 23:26:07 2026
tree
352e3f47587d09aadd076d321acda32c042501dd
parent
0558aa3f7a2448029d90e29e14916ca80789203b [ diff ] 
[HiC] Make canvas break out of the enclosing decorating box

The rules for breaking out of the decorating box for text-decoration
are specified at:

https://drafts.csswg.org/css-text-decor-4/#line-decoration

Canvas elements don't qualify because they are display:inline rather
than display:inline-block. However, it makes little sense to include
canvas contents in the enclosing decorating box, and it also leaks
visited link colors.

Fixed: 541436684

470aafad67f91b8060c159d8d444c6e93ce3f5f1 [ diff ] 
[BottomBar] Wire education opt-in dialog and pending tooltips handover

Wires BottomBarEducationDialogCoordinator into the startup promo flow
when the bottom bar is eligible but disabled and the education flow is
enabled. ToolbarManager#enableBottomControls() creates the dialog
instead of the bottom bar, and the new
ToolbarManager#maybeShowBottomBarPromo() gives
TabbedRootUiCoordinator#maybeShowPromo() one entry point for both
states: the bottom bar's own promo (via BottomBarHostManager) when the
bar is on, or the opt-in dialog when it is off. BottomBarHostManager is
unchanged and is still only created when the bottom bar is enabled.

ChromeTabbedActivity also observes BOTTOM_BAR_ENABLED on eligible
devices when the education flow is enabled (see
BottomBarConfigUtils#shouldObserveBottomBarEnabledChanges()), so tapping
"Try it now" recreates the activity with the bottom bar.

Also connects the cross-activity BOTTOM_BAR_EDUCATION_PENDING_TOOLTIPS
preference handover in BottomBarEducationCoordinator and
BottomBarMediator so the tooltip chain starts automatically after the
activity recreates.

BottomBarEducationCoordinator now picks the bubble's Gemini-focused copy
via BottomBarEducationUtils#shouldUseGeminiVariant(), which also
requires Gemini to be the profile's eligible extra action.

[BottomBar] Add BottomBarEducationDialogCoordinator for opt-in dialog

Introduces BottomBarEducationDialogCoordinator to display the modal
opt-in education dialog when the bottom bar is off by default and the
user has not explicitly set the preference. Tapping "Try it now" sets
ChromePreferenceKeys.BOTTOM_BAR_EDUCATION_PENDING_TOOLTIPS and enables
the bottom bar. Also adds the "Not now" and "Try it now" strings,
BottomBarConfigUtils#hasUserSetBottomBarEnabled() and
BottomBarEducationUtils#shouldUseGeminiVariant(), and makes
BottomBarConfigUtils#isBottomBarEligible() public.

Adds a 3-step sequential tooltip chain (Gemini -> New tab -> Tab
switcher) and its strings to BottomBarEducationCoordinator, triggered
when the user taps "Show me" on the education bubble. Each step
highlights the target button and advances on dismissal.

[Topics] Store fishfood feedback on collections

Collection ratings and flagged pages were only kept in the page, so
they were lost on reload. Save them in TopicFeedback like the other
feedback, and persist them in prefs. Collection titles are now kept
verbatim, since they identify the collection.

Part 2 of 3, follows crrev.com/c/8590741. Exporting the feedback is in
crrev.com/c/8591060.

Bug: b:568422896

Test: browser_tests --gtest_filter=ContextHubBrowserTest.Topics

base: Avoid redundant map lookups and temporary string keys

Use base::InsertOrAssign() in CommandLine::AppendSwitchNative() and
flat_map::insert_or_assign() in SequenceLocalStorageMap::Set() to avoid
redundant lookups and temporary std::string allocations when inserting
or updating map entries.

parent
5a654d77366883fca19664d8d2f128a130d91a44 [ diff ] 
[MenuSimplification] Prevent duplicate Reading Mode context menu entries

In RenderViewContextMenu, Reading Mode
(IDC_CONTENT_CONTEXT_OPEN_IN_READING_MODE) could be appended more than
once in two edge cases:
1. In the legacy/disabled menu simplification path with text selection
   in an editable field, AppendOtherEditableItems() appends reading mode
   first, followed by the ITEM_GROUP_SMART_SELECTION block in
   InitMenu().
2. In the MenuSimplification path when the
   ReadAnythingMenuShuffleExperiment arm kPlaceAtBottom is active,
   AppendPageItems() appends reading mode, and InitMenu() subsequently
   appends it again at the bottom of the menu.

This CL prevents duplicate items by:
1. Adding an idempotency guard at the entry of AppendReadAnythingItem()
   to check if IDC_CONTENT_CONTEXT_OPEN_IN_READING_MODE is already
   present.
2. Skipping the kPlaceAtBottom hook in InitMenu() when
   took_simplified_page_items_path is true, since simplified page items
   already append reading mode.

Unit tests are added covering both scenarios.

tree
cd980fce928b741c9e7516d5251640b3707aade5
parent
ca6613f49214577f1c3adc5e0bf1d519ce95e41d [ diff ] 
Deduplicate synchronous AppTask Binder IPCs in multiwindow startup

During ChromeTabbedActivity startup and shutdown, MultiWindowUtils and
MultiInstanceState call AndroidTaskUtils.getTaskInfoFromTask(task)
repeatedly for the same AppTask.

Pass RecentTaskInfo into isAppTaskMatchingType() so each loop queries
getTaskInfoFromTask() once per AppTask, and skip isRelevantTaskId() in
MultiInstanceState when mActiveTasks already determines the outcome.
In chrome_public_test_apk on an emulator, this reduces getAppTasks +
getTaskInfo calls per BlankCTATabInitialStateRule test from 10 to 4
(-60%) and saves ~11.6 ms per test.

Bug: 573931711
Change-Id: I07bb2f776545e826ae2ddd422d5e21acc65f8a5b

Reland "Avoid creating unnecessary GLContext on DrDC thread"

This is a reland of commit 90f7ea34ece3edc178307e25b4ef4202eaa04023

Contains additional null checks in SharedContextState destructor

Original change's description:
> Avoid creating unnecessary GLContext on DrDC thread
>
> On Android, the GLContext is never used on DrDC thread (no WebGL/Video).
>
> Update shared code, mainly in SharedContextState and shared image to
> allow null GLContext and associated classes like gles2::FeatureInfo.
>
> Added SharedContextState::GetMaxArrayTextureLayers (next to existing
> GetMaxTextureSize)
>
> Update webview and drdc init code to not create GLContext when not using
> GL, behind a feature.
>

d38b92d6e89948b5e80e4ea602c9e94584d88fc9 [ diff ] 
[NTP] Serve the Google logo from chrome://resources

<ntp-logo> loaded the Google logo from chrome://new-tab-page/icons/
google_logo.svg, which chrome://new-tab-page remapped at runtime to the
2026 version of the logo in Chrome-branded builds when
ntp_features::kNtpGoogleLogo26 is enabled. This tied the element to the
chrome://new-tab-page data source.

Chrome-branded builds already ship the current logo in chrome://resources
(cr_components/searchbox/icons/google_logo.svg, byte-identical to the
NTP copy). This CL:

- Serves the public new_tab_page/icons/google_logo.svg at that same
  chrome://resources path in non-branded builds, as is done for
  camera.svg and mic.svg. The file is read from its existing location
  rather than moved, so the trademarked image isn't added anywhere new
  in the public repo. It is no longer packaged in chrome://new-tab-page.
- Serves the 2026 logo in branded builds at
  cr_components/searchbox/icons/google_logo_26/google_logo.svg, instead
  of from the NTP's own grdp.
- Has logo.css pick the logo via the existing use-google-logo26_
  attribute, which reflects the useGoogleLogo26 loadTimeData value
  (always false in non-branded builds), and removes the runtime resource
  override from NewTabPageUI.

This removes a duplicate copy of the logo from branded desktop builds
and lets <ntp-logo> render the logo regardless of its host. No intended
visual change.

1ad887df7eb2b9c133a8782467e2c58b3fec3c79 [ diff ] 
[heap] Add fieldtrial testing config for V8LimitBasedMemoryReducer

This adds SuppressDetachCheck() in ClipboardPromise to fix
async-navigator-clipboard-write-multiple.tentative.https.sub.html
The failure is not new [1], but becomes deterministic with the feature
because of GC timing.
ClipboardPromise and its script_promise_resolver_ are kept alive solely by PromiseAll::WaitForAll waiting on user-supplied ClipboardItem promises, so if those JS promises become unreachable without settling, script_promise_resolver_ is legitimately garbage-collected while still pending.

[1] https://ci.chromium.org/ui/test/chromium/%3A%2F%2F%5C%3Aheadless_shell_wpt!webtest%3A%3Aexternal%2Fwpt%2Fclipboard-apis%23async-navigator-clipboard-write-multiple.tentative.https.sub.html?q=V%3Abuilder%3Dmac-rel+V%3Aos%3DMac-26+V%3Atest_suite%3Dheadless_shell_wpt_tests

📋 Metadata

tree
38d41c640d92d384a8a9fe134169c0f387e91abb
parent
5926523bcfedb77ba2533ace1dd93b477673f72a [ diff ] 
Activate inactive bubble subwindows on Wayland

On Wayland, bubble widgets use accelerated platform subsurfaces (via
kOzoneBubblesUsePlatformWidgets) that do not receive independent
activation state from the OS compositor. When a bubble is initially
displayed via ShowInactive() so it does not steal focus from the
browser window, clicking or focusing inside the bubble activates its
Aura content window without forwarding activation to the platform
window. As a result, the bubble never becomes the active bubble on the
parent window, causing subsequent Escape key presses to be routed to
the browser window and preventing close-on-deactivate dismissal when
clicking back outside.

Forward platform activation when Aura activates an inactive child
bubble host, matching the existing activation transfer when Aura
activates a parent host with child windows. On platforms where a
parented desktop bubble is a top-level window rather than a subsurface,
the extra Activate() call is redundant and harmless.

TAG=agy

Bug: 574559066
Cq-Include-Trybots: luci.chromium.try:linux-wayland-weston-rel

extensions: Enable exit-time destructor checks in test support

Remove the no_exit_time_destructors exemption from
//extensions:test_support and //extensions/browser:test_support.
TestExtensionsClient holds its permission message provider as a member,
GetTestPingData() returns by value, and LoggingTimer's global tracker
keeps its few keys in a fixed array, so it is trivially destructible.

[BottomBar] Wire BottomBarEducationCoordinator into BottomBarCoordinator

Plumbs BrowserControlsVisibilityManager, ScrimManager, the control
container and the bottom app bar container from ToolbarManager through
BottomBarContainerCoordinator into BottomBarCoordinator, which uses them
to instantiate BottomBarEducationCoordinator when the education param is
enabled.

Wires the education coordinator into BottomBarMediator to claim the
startup promo slot, trigger the bubble when visible on non-incognito
tabs, and dismiss when the bottom bar hides.

8b6bc62d09552aa7373d0a44b742dd370bf82082
parent
1daa08373ec834c9c1f6ef57bb1c656a5cf485a2 [ diff ] 
[a11y-android] Drop selection events from unfocused frames

The selection exposed to Android on the main frame root strictly
represents the selection of the focused frame. Previously, selection
changes in an unfocused frame still triggered document selection changed
events, causing spurious event dispatches and cache invalidations on the
root node.

This change returns early in FireDocumentSelectionChangedEvent when the
event's manager is not the manager for the focused frame.

AX-Relnotes: n/a
Bug: 481134054

[BottomBar] Handle rotation and controls position in education bubble

Updates BottomBarEducationCoordinator to observe configuration and
browser controls position changes. Re-shows the education bubble after
orientation changes without re-requesting the scrim or controls token,
and dismisses the bubble if the controls position changes.

tree
39f35ea946bb65725b867fcde9ec3a9cb5b17c7f
parent
efee6c17e9ab6e06c91d3d7efbc21755cdf4743e [ diff ] 
[a11y-canvas] Filter empty attributes and expand pointer/touch UKM listeners

Filter empty ARIA and title attributes in HTMLCanvasAccessibilityManager,
and expand CanvasRenderingContext HasMouseListener UKM telemetry to
include modern Pointer and Touch event types.

1. HTMLCanvasAccessibilityManager::ComputeHeuristicResult:
   Previously used FastHasAttribute for role, aria-label,
   aria-labelledby, aria-describedby, and title. Web frameworks (e.g.
   React, Vue) commonly render empty attributes like <canvas role=""
   aria-label="">, which caused the heuristic to incorrectly treat the
   canvas as having author accessibility attributes. The check now
   verifies that attribute strings are non-empty after trimming.

2. CanvasRenderingContext::RecordUKMCanvasAccessibility:
   mouse_event_types now includes pointer and touch interaction
   lifecycle events (pointerdown, pointerup, pointermove, pointercancel,
   pointerover, pointerout, touchstart, touchmove, touchend,
   touchcancel) in addition to legacy mouse events. Canvases binding
   only to modern pointer or touch event handlers are now accurately
   recorded with HasMouseListener = 1.

efee6c17e9ab6e06c91d3d7efbc21755cdf4743e

tree
620e0f13c2bbdfd8e5b659dbc3cc343a22293cef
parent
6bae21868153442a2ac22f1fc3efb30557f83bef [ diff ] 
Wait for the fragment parser to stop parsing when closing HTMLStream

Align HTMLStream's close algorithm with the spec
(whatwg/html#12758): instead of resolving the close() promise
immediately after calling Finish(), wait until the fragment parser
stops parsing in HTMLDocumentParser::PrepareToStopParsing().

When the stream is blocked on a parser-blocking classic external script, `Finish()` marks EOF on the input stream and delays ending the parser
until the script finishes and any remaining buffered input has been
parsed. Running the close callback in PrepareToStopParsing() (just
before AttemptToRunDeferredScriptsAndEnd()) ensures that close()
resolves after all pending parser-blocking scripts have executed and all
written input is in the DOM, while still resolving before defer and
module scripts run in subsequent tasks.

aaa38ba585d3da6712db051e8ae293a42061abc2
parent
f4b847b1b79f60a560ee892db61ce85c0fd7e495 [ diff ] 
[iOS] Support dynamic GPC eligibility changes for extensions

Universal Opt-Out / GPC eligibility can change dynamically at runtime.
Previously, IsGPCExtensionEnabled() and ExtensionServiceImpl::Initialize()
assumed eligibility was static at startup. If a user was ineligible at
launch, ExtensionService was either not created or exited early without
setting up preference observers.

This CL updates ExtensionService to be created and initialized even if the
user is currently ineligible, ensuring preference change observers are
active. The extension itself is still only loaded when the feature is
actually enabled.

[PM] Rename execution_context_priority to priority

The voting system no longer only deals with execution contexts (frames
and workers). It now also handles page votes and process votes, so the
"execution_context_priority" name is misleading.

This CL:
- Moves components/performance_manager/execution_context_priority/,
  components/performance_manager/public/execution_context_priority/
  and chrome/browser/performance_manager/execution_context_priority/
  to priority/ folders.
- Renames the performance_manager::execution_context_priority
  namespace to performance_manager::priority.
- Renames execution_context_priority.{h,cc,_unittest.cc} to
  priority.{h,cc,_unittest.cc}, and the ExecutionContextPriorityTest
  suite to PriorityTest.

No behavior change.

Bypass-Check-License: Moving existing files

Ash: Make the camera cancel upload dialog own its widget

This changelist is part of an ongoing series to move Ash widgets off
the deprecated NATIVE_WIDGET_OWNS_WIDGET and WIDGET_OWNS_NATIVE_WIDGET
ownership modes and onto CLIENT_OWNS_WIDGET, the mode views recommends
for all widgets. With it, the code that creates a widget owns it and
decides when it is destroyed. This removes a common source of dangling
pointers and window teardown bugs.

The camera cancel upload dialog now owns its widget instead of holding
a weak pointer to it, and destroys it when the dialog is closed or all
uploads finish.

Ash: Migrate WebAppPolicyManagerDisableListTest off RunUntilIdle

This changelist is part of an ongoing series to modernize Ash tests by
migrating them away from RunUntilIdle() and toward targeted
synchronization with APIs such as RunUntil(), TestFuture, RunLoop
callbacks, observer waiters, or exact mock-time advancement.

WebAppPolicyManagerDisableListTest no longer drains tasks after updating
the SystemFeaturesDisableList preference. The local-state test pref
service calls the policy observer synchronously, so the manager's
disabled system-app sets are already updated when the setter returns.
The assertions read those sets and the current disable-mode preference,
not the app records changed by the separately scheduled commands.

Ash: Migrate PolicyUIStatusTest off RunUntilIdle

This changelist is part of an ongoing series to modernize Ash tests by
migrating them away from RunUntilIdle() and toward targeted
synchronization with APIs such as RunUntil(), TestFuture, RunLoop
callbacks, observer waiters, or exact mock-time advancement.

PolicyUIStatusTest already waits in ReadStatusFor() for the new page's
status section to be shown, which only happens after the page receives
policy status. The helper reads the rendered fields after a renderer
idle callback, so a browser-thread idle drain adds no completion
guarantee. Remove it and keep the user and device timestamp assertions
unchanged.

[AF] Fetch OAuth2 token from IdentityManager cache
📋 Metadata

21a6046377d16aff4b0d65d641df7d2484776940
[ log ]
author

tree
11b12322d37e93a4c7f5691082234cfad44f7833
parent
d464dd75188c812ba946499844f214d77dae2ae1 [ diff ] 
[AF] Fetch OAuth2 token from IdentityManager cache

Currently, `PaymentsNetworkInterfaceBase` caches the last fetched OAuth2
token in `access_token_` and reuses it across requests without checking
for expiration. This could result in stale tokens sent in requests.

Instead, always call `StartTokenFetch(/*invalidate_old=*/false)` in
`IssueRequest()` so that token caching and expiration are delegated to
`IdentityManager`.

For more info, see go/gdfsc-missing-gaia.

Ash: Migrate SearchTagRegistryTest off RunUntilIdle

This changelist is part of an ongoing series to modernize Ash tests by
migrating them away from RunUntilIdle() and toward targeted
synchronization with APIs such as RunUntil(), TestFuture, RunLoop
callbacks, observer waiters, or exact mock-time advancement.

SearchTagRegistryTest now waits for each committed tag update before
checking the index size over a separate connection. The size check waits
for its own reply, while the existing observer assertions still verify
that each update produces exactly one notification.

Test: CQ

Update DownloadBubbleInteractiveUiTest for WebUI pinned toolbar actions

- Map kActionShowDownloads to kToolbarDownloadButtonElementId in
  pinned_toolbar_actions::GetElementIdentifierForAction() so the Views
  PinnedActionToolbarButton registers kToolbarDownloadButtonElementId on
  creation (matching WebUI's ActionIdToElementIdentifier mapping).
- Replace DownloadsVisibleObserver and direct
  PinnedToolbarActionsContainer access in
  DownloadBubbleInteractiveUiTest with WaitForShow and WaitForHide on
  kToolbarDownloadButtonElementId.
- Pass InputType::kKeyboard to PressButton in ClosePartialBubbleOnClick
  to avoid HighlightTracker's 100ms mouse-click suppression window
  immediately after clicking outside to dismiss the partial bubble.

Add "Close side panel" option to ephemeral button menu.

This adds a feature parameter to
kContextualTasksEphemeralButtonContextMenu to allow displaying "Close
side panel" instead of "Hide side panel" in the contextual tasks
ephemeral toolbar button context menu. Flag variations, strings, and
unit/interactive tests are included.
